- **fin-libraries**: One skill per library - fine-grained retrieval for 24 of the most-used Python quant packages, each leading with the single default or bug that silently produces wrong numbers. OPT-IN AND EXPENSIVE: measured at ~5,298 tokens of skill-listing budget on its own, and 24 skills already exceeds the ~20 at which Claude Code starts silently dropping descriptions to name-only. Set skillListingBudgetFraction to 0.03 before installing it, 0.05 to run it alongside fin-core, and 0.06 for every plugin. ([howard-lynn-ye/fin-skills](https://github.com/howard-lynn-ye/fin-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/fin-libraries) · ★1 · 24 skills)
- **fin-llm**: LLM- and ML-for-finance: agent frameworks (TradingAgents, FinRobot, RD-Agent, FinGPT), reinforcement learning and deep learning for trading, finance MCP servers, and the published evidence on whether any of it survives lookahead leakage, transaction costs, and multiple-testing correction. One skill per topic, source-verified, each with an executable script and the measured size of the trap it prevents. ([howard-lynn-ye/fin-skills](https://github.com/howard-lynn-ye/fin-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/fin-macro) · ★1 · 5 skills)
